([SteveGJones/jack-tar-deckhand](https://github.com/SteveGJones/jack-tar-deckhand/blob/HEAD/.archon/commands/bsa-plan.md) · ★3)

Use compliance-auditor (governance gate), solution-architect (architectural soundness), and documentation-architect (info-architecture review). ([SteveGJones/jack-tar-deckhand](https://github.com/SteveGJones/jack-tar-deckhand/blob/HEAD/.archon/commands/bsa-review.md) · ★3)
- **bsa-synthesise**: Consolidate plan + draft + validate + review into a decision-ready report for the speaker. You are NOT making editorial changes — the BSA work is done. You are summarising what shipped and what the speaker needs to decide. ([SteveGJones/jack-tar-deckhand](https://github.com/SteveGJones/jack-tar-deckhand/blob/HEAD/.archon/commands/bsa-synthesise.md) · ★3)

- **buddy:consolidate**: Consolidate accumulated memories — merge near-duplicates, archive stale entries, summarize tag-clusters, surface contradictions for resolution. Runs as a four-phase pipeline (rules shortlist → specialist judgment → user dry-run gate → apply). Pass a target (specialist alias, `common`, `all`) or one of the sub-commands `apply`/`revise &lt;text&gt;`/`cancel`. With no argument, consolidates memories of currently active specialists. ([mareurs/claude-plugins](https://github.com/mareurs/claude-plugins/blob/HEAD/buddy/commands/consolidate.md) · ★6)
- **buddy:create**: Create a new buddy specialist (global or project scope; builtin is frozen). Brainstorms with the user, drafts from the canonical skill template, previews what /buddy:summon will load (template + memory protocol + gates), then writes. Discovery is path-scan — no registration step needed. The argument is a freeform hint (e.g. "buddy for X", "buddy for X project") or empty. ([mareurs/claude-plugins](https://github.com/mareurs/claude-plugins/blob/HEAD/buddy/commands/create.md) · ★6)
- **buddy:introspect**: Run mid-session introspection on one or all currently summoned specialists. Unlike `/buddy:dismiss`, the specialist stays active afterward. Pass a specialist name to introspect just that one (e.g. `/buddy:introspect hamsa`); pass nothing to introspect all active. ([mareurs/claude-plugins](https://github.com/mareurs/claude-plugins/blob/HEAD/buddy/commands/introspect.md) · ★6)
